English language proficiency test scores (if applicable) Submitting an English proficiency test score (TOEFL, IELTS or TOEIC) is not mandatory. Your English language skills are also assessed in the selection interview. You will receive a waiver for the English proficiency test if English is your native language or if you have completed a university degree completely taught in English. If required, ESMT expects a minimum score of 100 in the TOEFL, 7.0 in the IELTS, or 850 in the TOEIC. The ESMT TOEFL institution code is 8707. Entrance exam (if applicable) - depending on your profile, you may be required to take the BAT or GMAT/GRE test GMAT or GRE scores are not obligatory if candidates can provide alternative evidence of quantitative skills or scientific training within their Bachelor studies through competitive grades, that included general quantitative courses or with a degree in engineering, natural sciences, mathematics, statistics, or another quantitative field (including economics or business with mainly theoretical/quantitative coursework). If you are completing your undergraduate studies within the next year, your application will also be considered. Ideally, applicants to the MAAI program will have between 0-3 years of post-graduate work experience . Applicants with up to five years of post-graduate work experience will be considered for admission on a case-by-case basis, following assessment and alignment of their academic and professional goals to the respective program. English language proficiency test scores (if applicable) Submitting an English proficiency test score (TOEFL, IELTS or TOEIC) is not mandatory. Your English language skills are also assessed in the selection interview. You will receive a waiver for the English proficiency test if English is your native language or if you have completed a university degree completely taught in English. If required, ESMT expects a minimum score of 100 in the TOEFL, 7.0 in the IELTS, or 850 in the TOEIC. The ESMT TOEFL institution code is 8707. Evidence of strong quantitative skills - depending on your profile, you may be required to take the BAT or GMAT/GRE test) GMAT or GRE scores are not obligatory if candidates can provide alternative evidence of quantitative skills or scientific training within their Bachelor studies through competitive grades, that included general quantitative courses or with a degree in engineering, natural sciences, mathematics, statistics, or another quantitative field (including economics or business with mainly theoretical/quantitative coursework). As a guideline, GMAT scores below 600 or GRE scores below 304 are not considered competitive. Depending on educational background and the grades, the review of scores can focus more on the quantitative section"

English language proficiency test scores (if applicable) Submitting an English proficiency test score (TOEFL, IELTS or TOEIC) is not mandatory. Your English language skills are also assessed in the selection interview. Recently graduated, with no more than 24 months of postgraduate work experience (excluding internships) English language proficiency test scores (if applicable) Submitting an English proficiency test score (TOEFL, IELTS or TOEIC) is not mandatory. Your English language skills are also assessed in the selection interview. You will receive a waiver for the English proficiency test if English is your native language or if you have completed a university degree completely taught in English. If required, ESMT expects a minimum score of 100 in the TOEFL, 7.0 in the IELTS, or 850 in the TOEIC. The ESMT TOEFL institution code is 8707. Evidence of quantitative skills - depending on your profile, you may be required to take the BAT or GMAT/GRE test) GMAT or GRE scores are not obligatory if candidates can provide alternative evidence of quantitative skills or scientific training within their Bachelor studies through competitive grades, that included general quantitative courses or with a degree in engineering, natural sciences, mathematics, statistics, or another quantitative field (including economics or business with mainly theoretical/quantitative coursework). As a guideline, GMAT scores below 600 or GRE scores below 304 are not considered competitive. Depending on educational background and the grades, the review of scores can focus more on the quantitative section of the respective test. Candidates may also take the ESMT Business Admissions Test (BAT) or receive a waiver for the tests if their academic transcripts or related qualifications demonstrate sufficient quantitative skills."

Applicants with up to five years of post-graduate work experience will be considered for admission on a case-by-case basis, following assessment and alignment of their academic and professional goals to the respective program. English language proficiency test scores (if applicable) Submitting an English proficiency test score (TOEFL, IELTS or TOEIC) is not mandatory. Your English language skills are also assessed in the selection interview. You will receive a waiver for the English proficiency test if English is your native language or if you have completed a university degree completely taught in English. If required, ESMT expects a minimum score of 100 in the TOEFL, 7.0 in the IELTS, or 850 in the TOEIC. The ESMT TOEFL institution code is 8707. Entrance exam (if applicable) - depending on your profile, you may be required to take the BAT or GMAT/GRE test GMAT or GRE scores are not obligatory if candidates can provide alternative evidence of quantitative skills or scientific training within their Bachelor studies through competitive grades, that included general quantitative courses or with a degree in engineering, natural sciences, mathematics, statistics, or another quantitative field (including economics or business with mainly theoretical/quantitative coursework). ESMT does not have a set minimum GMAT/GRE scores. As a guideline, GMAT scores below 600 (GMAT Focus below 555), or GRE scores below 304 are usually not considered competitive.
